# Branding and upstream-service review

The first-pass rebrand intentionally avoids mass-renaming internal Kotlin packages and service identifiers. Review every remaining match before a public release. Some matches are expected (license attribution, namespace, upstream URLs, comments, or internal property names); do not replace them without understanding their role.

- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 261: <string name="high_performance_battery_warning">पहले BitChord की बैटरी उपयोग सेटिंग को “अप्रतिबंधित” पर सेट करें। ज़्यादा रिफ़्रेश रेट ज़्यादा बैटरी, CPU और GPU इस्तेमाल करते हैं, और इससे आपका डिवाइस गर्म हो सकता है।</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 264: <string name="enable_high_performance_confirmation">इसे तभी चालू करें जब BitChord की बैटरी उपयोग सेटिंग “अप्रतिबंधित” पर हो। डिवाइस गर्म होने या पावर कम होने पर Android फिर भी रिफ़्रेश रेट सीमित कर सकता है।</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 299: <string name="discord_sign_in_footer">साइन इन करने पर Discord का लॉगिन पेज खुलता है। BitChord में कुछ भी टाइप नहीं किया जाता।</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 462: <string name="sources_order_footer">अगर किसी स्रोत के पास कोई ट्रैक नहीं है या उस तक पहुंचा नहीं जा सकता, तो BitChord अगला स्रोत आज़माता है। YouTube से ऊपर के स्रोत बेहतर वर्शन मिलने पर उसकी रिकॉर्डिंग बदल सकते हैं।</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 757: <string name="listen_together_official_server_dialog_message">यह पार्टी BitChord के आधिकारिक सर्वर पर चल रही है।\n\nक्या आप आधिकारिक सर्वर पर लौटकर पार्टी %1$s में शामिल होना चाहते हैं?</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 1435: <string name="bit_perfect_sheet_warning">ट्रैक को बिना बदलाव DAC पर भेजता है। इक्वलाइज़र, लाउडनेस नॉर्मलाइज़ेशन, क्रॉसफ़ेड, ऑटोमिक्स, प्लेबैक स्पीड और BitChord का वॉल्यूम बायपास हो जाते हैं।</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 1444: <string name="bit_perfect_mode_usb_direct_subtitle">BitChord किसी भी Android वर्शन पर DAC को खुद चलाता है और वॉल्यूम DAC पर ही सेट करता है। USB अनुमति माँगता है। सामान्य आउटपुट से काफ़ी तेज़ हो सकता है।</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-hi\strings.xml` line 1450: <string name="usb_allow_fixed_volume_confirm_message">BitChord इस तरह के DAC का वॉल्यूम सेट नहीं कर सकता, इसलिए यह फ़ुल स्केल पर चलता है और सिर्फ़ DAC का अपना नॉब, अगर हो, आवाज़ नियंत्रित करता है। संवेदनशील हेडफ़ोन पर यह ख़तरनाक रूप से तेज़ हो सकता है।</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-ja\strings.xml` line 1422: <string name="bit_perfect_mode_usb_direct_subtitle">どの Android バージョンでも BitChord が DAC を直接制御し、音量は DAC 側で設定します。USB の権限を求めます。通常の出力よりかなり大きな音になることがあります。</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ja\strings.xml` line 1428: <string name="usb_allow_fixed_volume_confirm_message">BitChord はこの種の DAC の音量を設定できないため、フルスケールで再生され、DAC 自体のつまみ（ある場合）でのみ音量を調節できます。感度の高いヘッドホンでは危険なほど大きな音になることがあります。</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 305: <string name="system_equalizer_footer">BitChord 설정 이후, 전체 오디오 세션에 적용됩니다. 두 설정 모두 쓰면 동시에 적용됩니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 408: <string name="high_performance_battery_warning">먼저 BitChord의 배터리 사용을 제한 없음으로 설정하세요. 주사율이 높을수록 배터리, CPU, GPU를 더 사용하며, 기기가 뜨거워질 수 있습니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 411: <string name="enable_high_performance_confirmation">BitChord의 배터리 사용을 제한 없음으로 설정한 경우에만 켜세요. 기기가 뜨겁거나 배터리 잔량이 적다면 Android에서 여전히 주사율을 제한할 수 있습니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 422: <string name="add_addon_detail">직접 호스팅하는 애드온 서버나 링크를 받은 서버를 BitChord에 지정하세요</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 454: <string name="discord_sign_in_footer">로그인 시 Discord의 로그인 페이지를 엽니다. 입력 정보가 BitChord로 넘어가지 않습니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 526: <string name="saved_to_bitchord_folder">Music/BitChord에 저장 완료</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 622: <string name="addon_url_description">애드온의 루트 경로나 manifest.json으로 가는 URL을 붙여넣으세요. BitChord에서 애드온이 검색하고 스트리밍할 수 있는 항목을 읽은 뒤, 평문으로 음악을 요청합니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 674: <string name="sources_order_footer">해당 소스에 음악이 없거나 연결할 수 없으면, BitChord에서 다음 소스 연결을 시도합니다. YouTube보다 상위에 있는 소스에서 더 나은 버전을 제공하는 경우 기존 음원을 교체할 수 있습니다.</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 752: <string name="replay_empty_description">조금 더 감상하시면 BitChord에서 기록을 리플레이로 만들어 드립니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 824: <string name="webdav_description">WebDAV 음악 폴더를 BitChord에 지정하세요 — Nextcloud 음악 경로나 WebDAV를 응답하는 아무 서버나 연결할 수 있습니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 841: <string name="smb_description">공유 음악 폴더를 BitChord에 지정하세요 — NAS 또는 SMB를 응답하는 Windows 공유 폴더나 연결할 수 있습니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 891: <string name="counted_on_device_with_bitchord">BitChord가 이 기기에서 집계</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 960: <string name="listen_together_share_text">BitChord에서 함께 들어요 — 파티 코드 %1$s</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 997: <string name="listen_together_official_server_dialog_message">이 파티는 공식 BitChord 서버에서 호스팅합니다.\n\n공식 서버로 되돌아가고 %1$s 파티에 참여하시겠습니까?</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 1151: <string name="source_needs_update">이 애드온은 오래된 버전입니다. 계속 사용할 수 있지만, 최신 BitChord 애드온 문서에 따라 업데이트하도록 개발자에게 요청하세요.</string>

- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 1286: <string name="bit_perfect_mode_off_subtitle">오디오가 Android 믹서와 BitChord 효과를 거칩니다.</string>
- `app\src\main\res\values-ko\strings.xml` line 1294: <string name="bit_perfect_mode_usb_direct_subtitle">어떤 Android 버전에서든 BitChord가 DAC를 직접 구동하고 음량을 DAC에서 설정합니다. USB 권한을 요청합니다. 일반 출력보다 훨씬 크게 들릴 수 있습니다.</string>

- `docs\web-app-build-prompt.md` line 3: You are building the web version of BitChord, an existing Android/desktop music player whose code is in this repository. - `web\app\src\playback\automix\NOTICE.md` line 65: - `engine/autodj.ts` follows BitChord's queue for the next track and hands

- `web\docs\apple-music-dom.md` line 14: **Font.** Decided for BitChord: **DM Sans from Google Fonts** (weights 400/500/600/700/800 plus italics, `display=swap`) is used everywhere, in place of the SF Pro / Inter stack from §2.2. Apple's type *scale* (sizes, weights, line heights) is kept. DM Sans is a little wider than SF Pro Text, so single-line labels that are truncated (lockup titles, LCD title) keep `text-overflow: ellipsis`.
- `web\docs\apple-music-dom.md` line 63: | Empty LCD | centred app glyph, 20×24 (BitChord uses its own mark) |
- `web\docs\apple-music-dom.md` line 76: A full-width red band (`--keyColor` background) is pinned at the bottom, above the player: a 2-line message, with a white "Try It Now" pill on the right. **BitChord uses this exact pattern for the "Install the extension" prompt** (decided in prompt §1).
- `web\docs\apple-music-dom.md` line 167: - **Theme override from the artwork.** Album and playlist pages set `--joe-color` (the artwork's background colour) on `.app-container.has-theme-override`, plus derived `--systemSecondary-vibrantOnDark-blended-plusL` (secondary text), `--systemTertiary-…` and `--opaqueShelfBG-blended-plusD` (alternate section background). It's dark or light layout (`container-detail-header--dark-layout`) depending on the artwork's luminance. BitChord takes the dominant colour from the thumbnail on the client to do the same.
- `web\docs\apple-music-dom.md` line 214: - The empty state is centred: "No lyrics available" 12px 600 secondary, then a 12px explanation. BitChord hosts **am-lyrics** here instead (prompt §10.2).
- `web\docs\apple-music-dom.md` line 235: - **Keyboard** (the web player's shortcuts, which BitChord matches): Space play/pause; ⌘/Ctrl+→ / ← next/previous; ⌘/Ctrl+↑ / ↓ volume; ⌘/Ctrl+F or `/` focuses search; Esc closes full screen or a modal.
- `web\docs\apple-music-dom.md` line 241: Apple also ships a set of overrides for browsers **without** `font: -apple-system-body` (everything except Apple devices). Because BitChord uses DM Sans on every platform, **we always use those overrides**: higher-contrast text alphas and a deeper key colour on light.
- `web\docs\automix.md` line 14: 12–18 s per track; cached in IndexedDB (`bitchord-automix`).
- `web\docs\kira-notes.md` line 35: ### What BitChord changes
- `web\docs\kira-notes.md` line 64: BitChord's Android version binds the token to the session's visitor data for the player request, and caches the tokens per session and visitor data (`innertube/potoken/`). Port that caching on top of this flow.
- `web\docs\kira-notes.md` line 79: - **Audio only for BitChord:** load the same SABR manifest but restrict Shaka to audio (`player.configure({ ... })` with video disabled, or filter the video adaptation sets out of the generated MPD). Prefer itag 251 (Opus), fall back to 140 (AAC).
- `web\docs\kira-notes.md` line 92: **Don't copy `adPlaybackContext: { pyv: true }`.** With it, googlevideo cuts every stream off at ~70 s (measured in phase 3; see `youtube-playback.md`). BitChord's player request leaves it out.
- `web\docs\lyrics.md` line 43: - The grant happens in the extension's tab, so when BitChord's tab comes back (focus or visibility) the app asks `lyrics.permissions` about the refused hosts. Any that are allowed now leave the notice, the lyric cache is dropped, and the lookup runs again.
- `web\docs\queue-and-player.md` line 52: Lengths: off, 3, 6, 9 or 12 seconds, from the ⋯ menu in Up Next. Shuffle, repeat, AutoPlay, volume, mute and crossfade persist in `localStorage` (`bitchord:player`).
- `web\docs\spotify-and-discord.md` line 55: - **The flag.** On for the site's zips. A Web Store build (`BITCHORD_STORE_BUILD=1`) leaves it out unless `DISCORD_PRESENCE=1`. Without it there's no `scripting` permission, no discord.com optional permission, no connect page, and no `discord` capability.
- `web\docs\spotify-and-discord.md` line 79: - **When it stops.** When the last BitChord page has been gone for 30 s, the card is cleared and the socket closed. A cleared card with nothing new for 5 minutes closes the socket too.
- `web\docs\youtube-signin.md` line 3: Prompt §5.3. There's no login screen: BitChord uses the YouTube session the browser already has, through the extension. The cookies and the signature never reach the page; it only gets names, pictures and InnerTube responses.
- `web\docs\youtube-signin.md` line 18: - **Logins:** a browser can hold up to ten Google logins, addressed by `X-Goog-AuthUser`, and the live ones needn't be contiguous. In the user's Chrome, 1, 2 and 4 were signed in and 0 and 3 had expired (`account_menu` answers 403 for those). So every index from 0 to 9 is tried, and a login counts only when `account_menu` returns an account header. `accounts_list` alone isn't trusted: it answers even for an expired index, with another login's channels. Taking that at face value made BitChord act as dead login 0, so everything came back signed out.
- `web\docs\youtube-signin.md` line 22: - **Disconnect** (sidebar account menu, or the popup) stops BitChord using the session. The user stays signed in to Google.
- `web\docs\youtube-signin.md` line 27: - On Chromium the page opens an `externally_connectable` port (`bitchord-events`).
